**Daisuke Ido**[ ([イド ダイスケ]{.t_nihongo_kanji
lang="ja"}[,]{.t_nihongo_comma style="display:none"} *[Ido
Daisuke]{.t_nihongo_romaji}*[^[[?]{.t_nihongo_icon
style="color:#00e;font:bold 80% sans-serif;text-decoration:none;padding:0 .1em;"}](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Help:Japanese "wikipedia:Help:Japanese"){.extiw}^]{.t_nihongo_help})]{style="font-weight: normal"}
is a [cyberphysician](/wiki/Cyberphysician "Cyberphysician") from
[Tiphares](/wiki/Tiphares "Tiphares"){.mw-redirect} and the first
[Tipharean](/wiki/Tipharean "Tipharean"){.mw-redirect} introduced in the
early half of *[Battle Angel
Alita](/wiki/Battle_Angel_Alita "Battle Angel Alita")*, being
responsible for reviving [Alita](/wiki/Alita "Alita") after finding her
in the [Scrapyard](/wiki/Scrapyard "Scrapyard"). Aside from being a
cyber physician, Ido also had a second line of work as a
[hunter-warrior](/wiki/Hunter-warrior "Hunter-warrior"){.mw-redirect}
while he lived in the [Scrapyard](/wiki/Scrapyard "Scrapyard"). He is
rarely called by his first name, mostly referred to by others as Dr.
Ido.

## [Appearance]{#Appearance .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=1 "Edit section: Appearance"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Appearance"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

Ido is a tall, fair-skinned man who appears to be in his mid-30s to
early 40s. His fair hair is always shown styled in a high brush cut and
he wears eyeglasses without temples. When he first appeared his hair was
bushier but this aspect was scaled back in later chapters. In the
[OVA](/wiki/Battle_Angel_(OVA) "Battle Angel (OVA)") and *[Gunnm:
Martian Memory](/wiki/Gunnm:_Martian_Memory "Gunnm: Martian Memory")*,
Ido is blond. He looks like a younger version of [Dr.
Russell](/wiki/Dr._Russell "Dr. Russell"){.mw-redirect}, suggesting that
they share the same genetic stock, a common occurrence on Tiphares. Like
all adult [Tiphareans](/wiki/Tipharean "Tipharean"){.mw-redirect}, he
bears a tattoo on his forehead signifying that he has undergone the
\"initiation\" required to become a citizen of Tiphares.

When working in his clinic, Ido wears a white lab coat with a dress
shirt, tie, and slacks. When he would go out or was working as a
hunter-warrior, he wore a black [trench coat]{.new
title="Trench coat (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvVHJlbmNoX2NvYXQ/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"}
and matching hat with a circular brim. The trench coat is brown in the
OVA. After he erased his memories and relocating to [Farm
21](/wiki/Farm_21 "Farm 21"), Ido practices full time as a
cyberphysician and appears to have stopped wearing the trench coat and
hat.

Ido\'s appearance is very similar to a character who appeared in [Yukito
Kishiro](/wiki/Yukito_Kishiro "Yukito Kishiro")\'s earlier doujinshi
*[Iron Fist](/wiki/Iron_Fist "Iron Fist")*. This character is a man with
glasses who wears a black hat and trench coat and wields a large hammer,
the titular iron fist, that he transports in a rolling case.

## [Personality]{#Personality .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=3 "Edit section: Personality"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Personality"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

Ido is an open, friendly and compassionate person, traits that serve him
well as a [cyberphysician](/wiki/Cyberphysician "Cyberphysician"),
although he is rarely called by his first name. He is also very curious,
as one of his hobbies involves visiting the
[Tiphares](/wiki/Tiphares "Tiphares"){.mw-redirect} dump heap and
searching for useful items. This curiosity led to him discovering the
[Berserker Body](/wiki/Berserker_Body "Berserker Body") and later
[Alita](/wiki/Alita "Alita").

Although he had to become a
[hunter-warrior](/wiki/Hunter-warrior "Hunter-warrior"){.mw-redirect} in
order to survive after arriving in the
[Scrapyard](/wiki/Scrapyard "Scrapyard"), Ido continues to pursue it as
a second line of work while practicing full time as a cyberphysician as
he grew to enjoy the thrill of the kill. He never shared this aspect of
his personality with anyone else.

When confronted with [Desty Nova](/wiki/Desty_Nova "Desty Nova")\'s
experiments on [humans](/wiki/Human "Human"), Ido sees Nova\'s actions
at first as hellish and repugnant. After he later discovers the
existence of the [brain
bio-chips](/wiki/Brain_bio-chip "Brain bio-chip") however, he begins to
gain some understanding of why Nova is undertaking his work. While his
psyche is strong enough not to lead him to commit suicide, it is not
strong enough to allow him to continue to live with the knowledge of
this truth. He thus possesses enough of a desire to continue living,
even if to do so involves the erasing of his memories.

While usually an honorable individual, it has been shown a few times
that Ido can be quite prideful and arrogant, but this can often lead to
him causing lingering rifts with his relationships with others; this is
most notably seen when Ido sides with Jashugan over Alita and even
belittles her in front of him with a smile on his face. While being held
hostage by [Zapan](/wiki/Zapan "Zapan"), it is revealed that Ido suffers
from acrophobia and is absolutely terrified of
heights.^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}1[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-1)^

## [Relationships]{#Relationships .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=4 "Edit section: Relationships"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Relationships"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

### [[Alita](/wiki/Alita "Alita")]{#Alita .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=5 "Edit section: Alita"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Alita"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

Ido and Alita\'s relationship is like that between a father and
daughter; Ido has stated that she is like a daughter to him more than
once^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}2[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-2)[[\[]{.cite-bracket}3[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-3)^
and considers her family, given that he reconstructed her and helped
\"raise\" her to adapt to life in the Scrapyard. The relationship
progresses through several stages reflecting Alita\'s mental and
emotional growth over time. Before he became aware of her [Panzer
Kunst](/wiki/Panzer_Kunst "Panzer Kunst") abilities and even briefly
after, Ido wanted Alita to remain pure and unsullied by not becoming a
hunter-warrior.^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}4[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-4)^.
He realized however, that her life was her own and accepted her
decision. Two years later, he accepted her decision to play
[motorball](/wiki/Motorball "Motorball") after discussing her
motivations with her. He also accepted Alita\'s willingness to \"leave
the nest\" so to speak, as after she retired from motorball she lived on
her own for two years. After being resurrected by [Desty
Nova](/wiki/Desty_Nova "Desty Nova") and learning the secret of
Tiphares, Ido left a last message for anyone looking for him in which
Alita was the only person mentioned by name. Because of his
[psychometry]{.new title="Psychometry (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvUHN5Y2hvbWV0cnk/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"},
[Kaos](/wiki/Kaos "Kaos") was able to read Ido\'s experiences when he
repaired Alita, turning Ido\'s parental love for her into a romantic
love.

### [[Kayna](/wiki/Kayna "Kayna")]{#Kayna .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=6 "Edit section: Kayna"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Kayna"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

Ido met [Kayna](/wiki/Kayna "Kayna") after he was resurrected by Nova. A
degree of trust was built between the two, such that Kayna accompanied
Ido after he fled the [Granite Inn](/wiki/Granite_Inn "Granite Inn") and
settled in [Farm 21](/wiki/Farm_21 "Farm 21"). Ido also entrusted Kayna
with showing anyone from his past who came searching for him a recording
he had made that explained that he had erased his memory. Kayna at some
point fell in love with Ido but kept the relationship professional for
the most part. She attempted to confess how she felt about him during
the [Battle of Farm 21]{.new
title="Battle of Farm 21 (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvQmF0dGxlX29mX0Zhcm1fMjE/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"}
but was interrupted. Ido was heartbroken at Kayna\'s death but after
learning that he had erased his memory, opted to fully regain his
memories prior to his death.

Other than the relationship that he formed with
[Carol](/wiki/Carol "Carol") years ago before the start of the main
story, Ido is not shown to have developed any other romantic
relationships. It is possible that his experience with Carol and her
death have prevented him from developing other romantic attachments, a
pattern that is later seen with the *[Battle Angel Alita: Last
Order](/wiki/Battle_Angel_Alita:_Last_Order "Battle Angel Alita: Last Order")*
characters [Arthur Farrell](/wiki/Arthur_Farrell "Arthur Farrell") and
[Aga Mbadi](/wiki/Aga_Mbadi "Aga Mbadi").
[Shumira](/wiki/Shumira "Shumira") is briefly interested in Ido out of
her appreciation for Ido rescuing her and aiding
[Jashugan](/wiki/Jashugan "Jashugan"), but this may also have been
motivated by her jealousy of Alita and was likely not reciprocated.
Desty Nova\'s assistant [Eelai](/wiki/Eelai "Eelai") takes an open
sexual interest in Ido largely because he is a \"full-flesh
man\"^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}5[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-5)^, but the
attraction was clearly one-sided and Ido expressed no interest in her.
Ido\'s relationship with [Chiren](/wiki/Chiren "Chiren") in the OVA is
implied to have been more than professional, as is suggested by the
[holocard](/wiki/Holocard "Holocard") that Chiren has of the two of them
together and Ido\'s reactions when he discovered that
[Vector](/wiki/Vector "Vector") had harvested her organs.

## [Abilities]{#Abilities .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=7 "Edit section: Abilities"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Abilities"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

**Medical doctor**: While in Tiphares, Ido received training as a
doctor. However, he struggled to earn a living after arriving in the
[Scrapyard](/wiki/Scrapyard "Scrapyard") due to the prevalence of
[cyborgs](/wiki/Cyborg "Cyborg") among its population, becoming a
[hunter-warrior](/wiki/Hunter-warrior "Hunter-warrior"){.mw-redirect}
out of necessity. While training with
[Dedekind](/wiki/Dedekind "Dedekind"), Ido\'s skill as a surgeon
increased to the point that he was offered and accepted a position as
Dedekind\'s partner. However following the deaths of Dedekind and
[Carol](/wiki/Carol "Carol"), Ido resumed his training as a
[cyberphysician](/wiki/Cyberphysician "Cyberphysician").

**Cyberphysician**: Two years after arriving in the Scrapyard, Ido began
training to become a cyberphysician under
[Gauss](/wiki/Gauss "Gauss").^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}6[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-6)^
After six months, though, Gauss told him that he lacked compassion for
cyborgs, without which he could never become successful. Following a six
month absence during which he trained under Dedekind, Ido returned to
complete his apprenticeship with Gauss, as his experiences had given him
the compassion to work with cyborgs. Following this, he became a highly
skilled cyberphysician in his own right and was able to open a
[clinic]{.new
title="Daisuke Ido's Scrapyard clinic (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvRGFpc3VrZV9JZG8lMjdzX1NjcmFweWFyZF9jbGluaWM/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"}
of his own.

After finding [Alita](/wiki/Alita "Alita") in the Tiphares dump heap,
Ido was able to revive her even though she had been dormant for 200
years. He subsequently built her first civilian body from various body
parts. After this body was destroyed in her first fight with
[Makaku](/wiki/Makaku "Makaku"), he supervised her transplantation into
the [Berserker Body](/wiki/Berserker_Body "Berserker Body") by
[Gonzu](/wiki/Gonzu "Gonzu"). With Gonzu\'s help, Ido later transplanted
[Hugo](/wiki/Hugo "Hugo")\'s head onto a cyborg body after he was nearly
killed by [Clive Lee](/wiki/Clive_Lee "Clive Lee"). Following Hugo\'s
death, Ido resuscitated [Jashugan](/wiki/Jashugan "Jashugan") after he
collapsed during [his Top League challenge race]{.new
title="Jashugan's Top League challenge race (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvSmFzaHVnYW4lMjdzX1RvcF9MZWFndWVfY2hhbGxlbmdlX3JhY2U/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"}
by using [Shumira](/wiki/Shumira "Shumira") to shock Jashugan\'s
[brain](/wiki/Brain "Brain") into reawakening. He briefly served as his
tuner and returned to his practice following Jashugan\'s death.

It is suggested that Ido briefly worked with [Desty
Nova](/wiki/Desty_Nova "Desty Nova") after being resurrected by him, as
Colonel [Bozzle](/wiki/Bozzle "Bozzle") referred to him as Nova\'s
right-hand man. When he discovered the existence of the Tipharean [brain
bio-chips](/wiki/Brain_bio-chip "Brain bio-chip"), Ido\'s psyche could
not handle the impact of this truth and he selectively erased his
memory, retaining only his memories and skills as a cyberphysician. He
left Nova and started a new life at [Farm 21](/wiki/Farm_21 "Farm 21")
with [Kayna](/wiki/Kayna "Kayna") as his nurse. It is a testament to the
degree of Ido\'s skill that [Kaos](/wiki/Kaos "Kaos"), who was able to
gain his abilities and experiences with Alita via his [psychometry]{.new
title="Psychometry (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvUHN5Y2hvbWV0cnk/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"},
was able to repair her [TUNED body](/wiki/TUNED_body "TUNED body"),
which Ido never worked on.

In the [OVA](/wiki/Battle_Angel_(OVA) "Battle Angel (OVA)"), Ido\'s
former partner [Chiren](/wiki/Chiren "Chiren") describes him as Zalem\'s
\"best cybernetics doctor ever.\" However, it is later revealed in the
manga that there are no cyborgs in Tiphares.

**Hunter-warrior**: Ido became a
[hunter-warrior](/wiki/Hunter-warrior "Hunter-warrior"){.mw-redirect}
out of necessity after arriving in the Scrapyard as he had not yet
learned how to become a cyberphysician. During this time he developed
the [rocket hammer](/wiki/Rocket_hammer "Rocket hammer") and became
skilled in its use. As he lacked any cybernetic enhancements, his
hunting style focused on ambushing his quarry, which if successful, did
not necessitate the use of the rocket hammer\'s booster. He was at a
disadvantage against cyborg opponents if the fight was prolonged or he
was disarmed.

After selectively erasing his memory and relocating to Farm 21, Ido
practiced as a full-time cyberphysician. However, after relearning about
his past from the recording he had made prior to his memory erasure and
later return to Tiphares, Ido found and used a sledgehammer to break
[Nova X](/wiki/Nova_X "Nova X")\'s hand and crush his foot. He fully
regained his memories after taking a pill given by Nova X, recalling his
hunter-warrior training.

**Melee combat**: Ido\'s use of the rocket hammer, while self-taught,
gave him a degree of ability in fighting with blunt weapons, including
pipes. He once fought and knocked out two thugs who were respectively
armed with a [kukri]{.new title="Kukri (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvS3Vrcmk/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"} and
chain using a piece of pipe he picked up.

**Great physical strength**: Ido possess a degree of physical strength
not evident with his tall stature, as he was able to wield the rocket
hammer as an effective melee weapon without activating its booster.
Despite not actively working as a hunter-warrior for a decade following
his death and regeneration by Desty Nova, Ido still retains his strength
as he skillfully wielded a sledgehammer against [Nova
X](/wiki/Nova_X "Nova X") without any evidence of strain.

## [Equipment]{#Equipment .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=8 "Edit section: Equipment"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Equipment"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

**[Rocket hammer](/wiki/Rocket_hammer "Rocket hammer")**: Ido\'s
trademark weapon as a hunter-warrior which he designed and built
himself. It was very effective against large, slow bounties and deadlier
still when the rocket in the head was activated. It was lost during
Alita\'s battle with [Zapan](/wiki/Zapan "Zapan").

**Pipes**: Used as an improvised weapon when he went looking for Alita
after she ran away from home following [Hugo](/wiki/Hugo "Hugo")\'s
death. Ido first picked up a long section to rescue two thugs who tried
to rape [Shumira](/wiki/Shumira "Shumira") before switching to a smaller
section that he could conceal in his trench coat.

**Sledgehammer**: Picked up on
[Tiphares](/wiki/Tiphares "Tiphares"){.mw-redirect} and used to cripple
[Nova X](/wiki/Nova_X "Nova X"). Ido later used the sledgehammer against
the [robots]{.new title="Robot (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvUm9ib3Q/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"} loyal to
[Hecaton]{.new title="Hecaton (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvSGVjYXRvbj9hY3Rpb249ZWRpdCZyZWRsaW5rPTE="}.

## [History]{#History .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=9 "Edit section: History"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: History"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

Ido was expelled from
[Tiphares](/wiki/Tiphares "Tiphares"){.mw-redirect} seven years before
the start of the main story, making it the year \"B.K. 7\" within the
[Anno Koyomi
chronology](/wiki/Koyomi_K.#Ages_and_story_chronology "Koyomi K."), or
ES 569 as per the [Era Sputnik](/wiki/Era_Sputnik "Era Sputnik") dating
convention introduced in *Last
Order*.^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}7[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-7)^ Ido\'s
background in Tiphares is not revealed in the manga, although his skills
as a regular doctor imply that this was his profession. The reasons for
his expulsion from Tiphares are not stated, but [Desty
Nova](/wiki/Desty_Nova "Desty Nova") states that on occasion, Tiphareans
like him and Ido will be created who lack a \"limiter\", which makes
them defects^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}8[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-8)^
within the Tipharean scheme of order and a potential danger to their
fellow citizens. Such elements are purged by being literally cast out
with the garbage onto the Scrapyard below.

Ido survived his fall, and for an unknown period of time was living with
a woman named [Dina](/wiki/Dina "Dina") in the Scrapyard. It was also
during this time that he acquired the original
Gally/[Alita](/wiki/Alita_(cat) "Alita (cat)"), a black cat. Due to
struggling to survive in an unknown environment, Ido became a
[hunter-warrior](/wiki/Hunter-warrior "Hunter-warrior"){.mw-redirect} in
order to survive as he had not yet learned how to become a
cyberphysician at this time. He develops his trademark [rocket
hammer](/wiki/Rocket_hammer "Rocket hammer") and wears a black hat and
trench coat during his hunts. Two years after his expulsion, he saved a
young woman from a massive cyborg, but was kicked out by Dina after
bringing her home. Ido and the young woman, who he named
[Carol](/wiki/Carol "Carol") as she appeared to have suffered
regression, then stayed with [Gonzu](/wiki/Gonzu "Gonzu") for a year.
During this time, Ido studied to become a cyberphysician under the
tutelage of the veteran [Gauss](/wiki/Gauss "Gauss") for six months, but
learned that he lacked the compassion to operate on cyborgs. Soon
afterwards, he encountered the Tipharean cyberphysician
[Dedekind](/wiki/Dedekind "Dedekind"), who specialized in replacing the
cyborg limbs of his patients with recloned ones grown from their DNA
using Tipharean technology. After working with Dedekind for six months,
Ido accepted his offer to become his partner.

Later that year, however, Ido discovered that Carol was actually a clone
of Dedekind\'s patient [Pakila](/wiki/Pakila "Pakila"){.mw-redirect}.
After the deaths of Carol, Pakila, and Dedekind, Ido found the purpose
he had been lacking and returned to complete his apprenticeship with
Gauss, eventually opening up his own clinic five years before the start
of the main story. His patients include the cyborg residents of the
Scrapyard as well as hunter-warriors like [Zapan](/wiki/Zapan "Zapan").
Ido also found the [Berserker
Body](/wiki/Berserker_Body "Berserker Body") sometime after in the
remnants of a crashed spaceship and took it, hiding it away in an
underground vault beneath his clinic after discovering the destructive
potential that it
possessed.^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}9[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-9)^

## [Plot]{#Plot .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=10 "Edit section: Plot"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Plot"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

### [*Battle Angel Alita*]{#Battle_Angel_Alita .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=11 "Edit section: Battle Angel Alita"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Battle Angel Alita"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

One day during his routine visit to the Scrapyard dump heaps, Ido\'s
discovery of [Alita](/wiki/Alita "Alita") marks the beginning of the
*Battle Angel Alita* story and occurred the same year that [Koyomi
K.](/wiki/Koyomi_K. "Koyomi K.") was born, making it Year 0 in the Anno
Koyomi chronology or ES 576. After reviving and transplanting her into a
civilian cyborg body, Ido tries to keep his bounty hunting secret from
Alita, but she eventually finds out and decides to become a
hunter-warrior herself. At first initially opposed to the idea, Ido
comes to accept it. He is nearly killed in Alita\'s first fight with
[Makaku](/wiki/Makaku "Makaku"), but is rescued and treated by Gonzu,
who under his supervision, transplants Alita into the [Berserker
Body](/wiki/Berserker_Body "Berserker Body"). Ido\'s decision to
introduce the newly rebuilt Alita to the other hunter-warriors at [Bar
Kansas](/wiki/Bar_Kansas "Bar Kansas"), a popular hunter-warrior
hangout, brings her into conflict with [Zapan](/wiki/Zapan "Zapan"), and
unwittingly sets in motion a series of events that sees him later emerge
as one of Alita\'s deadliest adversaries.

After Alita becomes involved [Hugo](/wiki/Hugo "Hugo") and he becomes a
bounty, Zapan uses Ido as bait in order to trap Alita, whom he suspects
of rebellion for trying to flee with Hugo. Rescued by Alita after she
defeats Zapan, Ido and Gonzu transplant Hugo\'s head onto a cyborg body.
At this point in the story, Ido reveals that it is impossible for anyone
from the surface to reach Tiphares, much less buy passage there. After
Alita runs away following Hugo\'s death, Ido spends a month on the
streets looking for her, ending up in the Western Region of the
Scrapyard. After rescuing [Shumira](/wiki/Shumira "Shumira"), the sister
of the reigning [motorball](/wiki/Motorball "Motorball") champion
[Jashugan](/wiki/Jashugan "Jashugan"), from a pair of thugs, Ido
discovers that Alita has become a motorball player and tries to get her
attention after a match that she wins, but she ignores him.

Ido stays with Shumira for a week, then witnesses Jashugan\'s first race
on the circuit in a month. During the race, Jashugan suffers a flatline
attack that forces him to pit. Ido ends up saving him by connecting
Shumira\'s brain waves to an amplifier, enabling him to come from behind
and win the
race.^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}10[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-10)^ He
joins Jashugan\'s team as a result, becoming his tuner with the hope of
forcing Alita to leave motorball and come home by helping Jashugan win
his challenge match with her. The day of the challenge match, Ido is
tasked by Jashugan to take care of Shumira and promises to not disgrace
the circuit.^[[\[]{.cite-bracket}11[\]]{.cite-bracket}](#cite_note-11)^
After Alita wins the match, he returns to his practice and sideline
occupation as a hunter-warrior.

Two years after this, Ido volunteers to buy back the Berserker Body from
Desty Nova, to whom it was traced to after Alita\'s former motorball
manager [Ed](/wiki/Ed "Ed"){.mw-redirect} sold it in an attempt to keep
her on the motorball circuit. This results in his meeting Nova, the
first time that he has encountered a fellow Tipharean face to face since
meeting [Dedekind](/wiki/Dedekind "Dedekind") years ago. Ido gets caught
up in Nova\'s plan to unite Zapan\'s brain with the Berserker Body, and
when Zapan in his newly reincarnated form starts to wreak havoc,
attempts to inject him with collapser to stop him, but gets killed in
the process, as he had not yet been injected with restorer nanomachines
that would have helped him physically regenerate. Nova promises Alita
that he will resurrect Ido as he has become his patient. Ido\'s clinic
is subsequently destroyed by Zapan in his search for Alita.

Alita does not encounter Ido again until 11 years later, when she is
working as a [TUNED](/wiki/TUNED "TUNED") agent. With
[Kaos](/wiki/Kaos "Kaos")\'s help, she learns that Ido is at [Farm
21](/wiki/Farm_21 "Farm 21"), which has been under
[Barjack](/wiki/Barjack "Barjack") control for two years. Here she finds
Ido, but he does not remember her. Ido\'s cyborg nurse
[Kayna](/wiki/Kayna "Kayna") shows Alita a recording that Ido made five
years prior which he made after discovering the [Secret of
Tiphares](/wiki/Secret_of_Tiphares "Secret of Tiphares"){.mw-redirect}
for himself after rebuffing repeated attempts by Nova to make him his
assistant. This revelation nearly drove Ido to madness, and he decided
to erase his memory rather than having to come to terms with the
implications of his newfound knowledge. Following this, he took work
again as a cyberphysician in Farm 21, where Alita was forced to leave
him.

### [*Last Order*]{#Last_Order .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=12 "Edit section: Last Order"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Last Order"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

Like several characters from *Battle Angel Alita*, Ido did not make an
active appearance for most of *Last Order* outside of appearing in
Alita\'s thoughts. However, following the end of the [Tenth Zenith of
Things
Tournament](/wiki/Tenth_Zenith_of_Things_Tournament "Tenth Zenith of Things Tournament")
and imminent crash of [Ketheres](/wiki/Ketheres "Ketheres") into
[Luna]{.new title="Luna (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvTHVuYT9hY3Rpb249ZWRpdCZyZWRsaW5rPTE="}, he and
[Figure Four](/wiki/Figure_Four "Figure Four") were shown traveling to
the Scrapyard.

A flashback to eleven months earlier shows Figure hearing word of
Alita\'s death during the failed attack on Tiphares during the [Barjack
War](/wiki/Barjack_War "Barjack War") (actually
[AR-10](/wiki/AR-10 "AR-10"), one of Alita\'s
[TUNED](/wiki/TUNED "TUNED")
[duplicates](/wiki/TUNED_AR_Series_2 "TUNED AR Series 2")). He travels
to the site of the attack and the molten remains of the railgun
[Heng](/wiki/Heng "Heng") and encounters another duplicate,
[AR-4](/wiki/AR-4 "AR-4"), whom he mistakes for Alita. She plays along
and asks for his help. Shortly afterwards, they are attacked by
[Sechs](/wiki/Sechs "Sechs"), who kills AR-4 and badly wounds Figure,
severing his right arm. Figure manages to flee into a river and drifts
unconscious to Farm 21 with AR-4 in his grasp. Barely alive, he is saved
by Ido and Kayna. They take him in and explain to him the true identity
of AR-4, who receives a proper burial. They also give Figure a cyborg
arm to replace his lost one and put him to work at the farm to pay it
off.

After Figure breaks his cyborg arm sparring against a tree, Ido and
Kayna give him another, more advanced (and more expensive) model, though
they let him put it on a tab so he can repay it when he can. While
recovering, Figure starts talking with Dr. Rivet, a cyborg and former
doctor of Barjack. He turns out to be running a cult based on fragments
of Nova\'s teachings. Having heard that Nova had two bodyguards, one
\"[superhuman behemoth](/wiki/Barzarld "Barzarld")\" and one \"[buxom
female aide](/wiki/Eelai "Eelai")\", he comes to the conclusion that Ido
*is* Nova. Figure confronts Ido with this claim. Since he has no
memories prior to his time at Farm 21 due to having wiped them, Ido
considers the possibility that he is Nova and erased his own memory to
hide. Kayna knows the real reason why and has the recording of him
revealing it to Alita but has vowed not to reveal it. The cult begins
spreading the rumor and carried by merchants across the land until it
reaches the town of Bashaku, where a businessman, Chairman Steed of the
company Desert Trading, who lost his daughter and her family to Nova\'s
experiments, puts a 80 million bounty on Ido and a 5 million bounty on
Figure and Kayna. Not long afterwards, a small, but a savage army of
mercenaries and bounty hunters show up at Farm 21, kicking down doors,
destroying houses and killing everyone they see looking for Ido. He
tries to end the carnage by revealing himself to them, but Kayna and
Figure stop him and knock him out. While Dr. Rivet helps them prepare to
flee, Kayna returns to the clinic, which has been set on fire by the
hunters, to save Ido\'s confession recording.

Dr. Rivet takes them to the cult\'s headquarters at the Sunflower
Nursery, an old windmill, where a wounded Chairman Steed is being held.
Ido tries to convince him that he isn\'t Nova and pleads him to call off
the hunters. He agrees, but as soon as they give him a radio, he
broadcasts their location, sending the lot of them their way. Ido, Kayna
and Figure prepare to flee in an armored Barjack car, but then the
windmill is attacked by an enormous, dome-headed cyborg looking for
Nova. After brushing off an attack by Kayna, he grabs Ido and confirms
to Ido, Dr. Rivet and Steed that he isn\'t Nova. Frustrated, he starts
wrecking the windmill. When he recognizes Kayna from her time working as
Nova\'s assistant, she tells Ido and Figure to run and gives Figure her
bag, containing the confession recording. At the windmill, the cyborg,
who is revealed to consist of three different people who were implanted
into the same cyborg body by Nova (the sister and one of the brothers
have both succumbed to madness, leaving the other in control of the
body), chases after Kayna. She is able to impale it with debris and kill
the two brothers before being destroyed herself as the windmill
collapses.

Meanwhile, Figure and Ido drive off to Bashaku with Dr. Rivet and Steed
in the backseat. In the desert, the cyborg, now controlled by the
sister, meets them and hands them Kayna\'s dead body before succumbing
to its injuries. After burying her, Ido and Figure turn themselves in to
the Bashaku town council and are brought to trial. They are shown the
tape of Ido\'s confession to Alita but suspect it\'s a clever ruse. They
are then shown a second recording made by Kayna herself, where she
asserts Ido\'s innocence. In the end, the council sentences Steed to
indefinite confinement until his punishment for starting the massacre
can be decided. For spreading the rumor that Ido was Nova, Rivet is sent
to a mental health facility for treatment. Unfortunately, Ido and Figure
are sentenced to death by firing squad as Nova\'s co-conspirators in a
move to put the rumor\'s of Nova\'s presence to rest.

While awaiting their executions, Ido and Figure try to escape by faking
Ido\'s suicide but fail. As they are about to be taken out and shot,
they are sprung from their cells by locals, who know of Ido\'s good
deeds and how he has worked to help the poor. They cover the escape,
blocking the Bashaku town guards from catching them, and give Ido and
Figure the supplies they need to make the trip through the desert.

Ido is a major character in the
[OVA](/wiki/Battle_Angel_(OVA) "Battle Angel (OVA)"). He was considered
the best cyberphysician in [Zalem](/wiki/Zalem "Zalem") and is the
former partner of the anime-only character
[Chiren](/wiki/Chiren "Chiren"), with whom it is implied he was also
personally involved with. Since the OVA story has differs significantly
from the manga however, this relationship is not considered canon.

The circumstances of [Gally](/wiki/Gally "Gally"){.mw-redirect}
obtaining the [Berserker Body](/wiki/Berserker_Body "Berserker Body")
were changed, as Ido transplants her into it after reviving her. He is
injured during the fight with
[Rasha](/wiki/Rasha "Rasha"){.mw-redirect}, but not as severely as he
was in the manga by [Makaku](/wiki/Makaku "Makaku"), as he does not
fight [Grewcica](/wiki/Grewcica "Grewcica"). Ido also singlehandedly
transplant\'s [Yugo](/wiki/Yugo "Yugo"){.mw-redirect}\'s head onto a
[cyborg](/wiki/Cyborg "Cyborg") body instead of being assisted by
[Gonz]{.new title="Gonz (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvR29uej9hY3Rpb249ZWRpdCZyZWRsaW5rPTE="}. After
realising that Yugo was duped by [Vector](/wiki/Vector "Vector"), he is
the one who confronts Vector about lying to Yugo, physically assaulting
him with the [rocket hammer](/wiki/Rocket_hammer "Rocket hammer"). When
[Zahriki](/wiki/Zahriki "Zahriki"){.mw-redirect} appears, Ido kills him
with the rocket hammer and his torso flies into and impales Vector. At
the end of [Tears Sign](/wiki/Tears_Sign "Tears Sign"), Ido and Gally
place Yugo\'s forearm and Chiren\'s earring into a basket which borne
into the air by a balloon towards Zalem.

### [*Martian Memory*]{#Martian_Memory .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=15 "Edit section: Martian Memory"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Martian Memory"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

Ido is an NPC for most of the game but briefly becomes a playable
character when visiting [Desty Nova\'s manor]{.new
title="Desty Nova's manor (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvRGVzdHlfTm92YSUyN3NfbWFub3I/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"}.
At the end of the game, which occurs five years later, he and
[Kayna](/wiki/Kayna "Kayna") are invited to Salem by Gally for the
departure of *[Leviathan I]{.new
title="Leviathan I (page does not exist)"
uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvTGV2aWF0aGFuX0k/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x"}*,
but he has not regained his memories of his life in the
[Scrapyard](/wiki/Scrapyard "Scrapyard").

### [*Alita: Battle Angel*]{#Alita:_Battle_Angel .mw-headline}[[\[]{.mw-editsection-bracket}[![](data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyBjbGFzcz0id2RzLWljb24gd2RzLWljb24tdGlueSIgYXJpYS1oaWRkZW49InRydWUiIGZvY3VzYWJsZT0iZmFsc2UiPjx1c2UgeGxpbms6aHJlZj0iI3dkcy1pY29ucy1wZW5jaWwtdGlueSI+PC91c2U+PC9zdmc+){.wds-icon .wds-icon-tiny}](https://battleangel.fandom.com/wiki/Daisuke_Ido?veaction=edit&section=16 "Edit section: Alita: Battle Angel"){.mw-editsection-visualeditor tracking-label="edit-section-anon" data-action="edit-section" aria-label="Edit section: Alita: Battle Angel"}[\]]{.mw-editsection-bracket}]{.mw-editsection}

In the live-action film *[Alita: Battle
Angel](/wiki/Alita:_Battle_Angel "Alita: Battle Angel")*, Ido is
portrayed by and his first name is changed to Dyson. He names Alita
after his deceased [daughter](/wiki/Alita_Ido "Alita Ido") with Chiren.
Unlike his manga counterpart, Dyson Ido is older in age, in his 50s or
60s and was previously married.
